EPS from continuing operations for the nine months ended </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">June&#160;29, 2019</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">decreased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$5.98</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;from </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$6.81</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in the prior-year period. Excluding certain items affecting comparability</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;"><sup style="vertical-align:top;line-height:120%;font-size:8pt">(1)</sup></font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">, EPS from continuing operations for the nine months </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">decreased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">15%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$4.75</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;from </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$5.60</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in the prior-year period. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:12px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#8220;Our third-quarter results reflect our efforts to effectively integrate the 21st Century Fox assets to enhance and advance our strategic transformation,&#8221; said Robert A. Iger, Chairman and Chief Executive Officer, The Walt Disney Company. &#8220;I&#8217;d like to congratulate The Walt Disney Studios for reaching $8 billion at the global box office so far this year--a new industry record--thanks to the stellar performance of our Marvel, Pixar and Disney films. The incredible popularity of Disney&#8217;s brands and franchises positions us well as we launch Disney+, and the addition of original and library content from Fox will only further strengthen our direct-to-consumer offerings.&#8221;</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:12px;text-indent:32px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">On March 20, 2019, the Company acquired Twenty-First Century Fox (21CF) for cash and the issuance of 307 million shares. style="text-align:left;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;"><br></font></div></td><td style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#160;%</font></div></td></tr></table></div></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Cable Networks</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:4px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Cable Networks revenues for the quarter </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">24%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$4.5 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and operating income </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">15%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$1.6 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">. Higher operating income was due to the consolidation of 21CF businesses (primarily the FX and National Geographic networks) and an increase at ESPN, partially offset by a decrease at Freeform.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The increase at ESPN was due to higher advertising and affiliate revenue, partially offset by an increase in programming and production costs. Higher advertising revenue was due to increases in units sold and rates, partially offset by lower viewership. Advertising revenue was positively impacted by two additional NBA finals games. Affiliate revenue growth was driven by contractual rate increases, partially offset by a decline in subscribers. The increase in programming and production costs was due to contractual rate increases for MLB and NBA programming and new rights for boxing and mixed martial arts.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The decrease at Freeform was due to an increase in programming and production costs, partially offset by higher income from program sales. The programming and production cost increase reflected the timing of amortization and higher average cost of programming in the current quarter.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Broadcasting</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:4px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Broadcasting revenues for the quarter </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">16%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$2.2 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and operating income decreased </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">17%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">to</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$307 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">. Lower operating income was due to decreases in ABC Studios program sales and network advertising revenue, partially offset by a decrease in programming costs, higher affiliate revenue and, to a lesser extent, the consolidation of 21CF businesses. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:4px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The decrease in ABC Studios program sales was driven by the prior year sale of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Luke Cage</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and lower sales of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">How to Get Away With Murder </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">and </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Designated Survivor.</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;The decrease in network advertising revenues reflected lower viewership, partially offset by higher rates. Lower programming costs reflected a decrease in the average cost of programming in the current quarter compared to the prior-year quarter, which included airings of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Roseanne,</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;as well as lower program cost write-downs. </font></div><div><br></div><div><div style="line-height:120%;text-align:center;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;">3</font></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div><a name="s3FDBBCC431A05E9FBC7E1E8FDE6D663D"></a></div><div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;"><br></font></div></div><div><br></div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;">Parks, Experiences and Products</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Parks, Experiences and Products revenues for the quarter </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">7%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$6.6 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and segment operating income </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">4%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$1.7 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">. Operating income growth for the quarter was due to increases at our consumer products businesses and Disneyland Paris, partially offset by a decrease at our domestic parks and resorts. Results included a benefit from a shift in the timing of the Easter holiday. In the current year, the entire Easter holiday fell in the third quarter, while the third quarter of the prior year included only one week of the Easter holiday. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The increase at our consumer products business was due to growth at our merchandise licensing and retail businesses. Growth at merchandise licensing was primarily due to higher revenue from merchandise based on Toy Story, partially offset by a decrease from Star Wars merchandise. The increase at our retail business was due to higher comparable store sales and online revenue. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Higher operating income at Disneyland Paris was primarily due to higher average ticket prices, partially offset by labor and other cost inflation and lower attendance. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The decrease in operating income at our domestic parks and resorts was due to higher costs and lower volume, partially offset by increased average per capita guest spending. Higher costs were driven by labor and other cost inflation and expenses associated with </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Star Wars</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">: Galaxy&#8217;s Edge, which opened at Disneyland Resort on May 31. The decrease in volume was due to lower attendance, partially offset by higher occupied room nights. Guest spending growth was primarily due to higher average ticket prices and increased food, beverage and merchandise spending.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;"><br></font></div><div><a name="s552624E72E6B564A9EFC87B4A325AECA"></a></div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;">Studio Entertainment</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:11pt;"></font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Studio Entertainment revenues for the quarter </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">33%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$3.8 billion</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and segment operating income </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">increased</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">13%</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$792 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">. Higher operating income was due to an increase in theatrical distribution results and lower film cost impairments at our legacy operations. These improvements were partially offset by a loss from the 21CF businesses and lower TV/SVOD and home entertainment distribution results at our legacy operations.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The increase in theatrical distribution results was due to the performance of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Avengers: Endgame, Aladdin, Captain Marvel</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Toy Story 4</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in the current quarter compared to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Avengers: Infinity War</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">, </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Incredibles 2, Black Panther</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Solo: A Star Wars Story </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">in the prior-year quarter. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Operating results at the 21CF businesses reflected a loss from theatrical distribution driven by the performance of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Dark Phoenix</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">, for which we also recorded a film cost impairment, partially offset by income from TV/SVOD distribution.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Lower TV/SVOD distribution results were due to sales of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Star Wars: The Last Jedi</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Thor: Ragnarok</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in domestic pay television in the prior-year quarter with no comparable titles in the current quarter.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The decrease in home entertainment results was due to lower unit sales and net effective pricing reflecting the performance of </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Black Panther</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in the prior-year quarter compared to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-style:italic;">Captain Marvel</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;in the current quarter. </font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;"><br></font></div><div><a name="s7372B7A3EB25547392FC8ABD28EF2D5D"></a></div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline;">Direct-to-Consumer &amp; International</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-bottom:8px;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Direct-to-Consumer &amp; International revenues for the quarter increased from </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$827 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$3,858 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;and segment operating loss increased from </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$168 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;to </font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">$553 million</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">. The increase in operating loss was due to the consolidation of Hulu, the ramp up of investment in ESPN+, which was launched in April 2018 and costs associated with the upcoming launch of Disney+. Results for the quarter also </font></div><div><br></div><div><div style="line-height:120%;text-align:center;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;">4</font></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;"><br></font></div></div><div><br></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-bottom:8px;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">reflected a benefit from the inclusion of the 21CF businesses due to income at the Fox and National Geographic international channels, partially offset by a loss at Star India.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-bottom:8px;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">Commencing on March 20, 2019, 100% of Hulu&#8217;s operating results are included in the Direct-to-Consumer &amp; International segment as a result of our acquisition of a controlling interest in Hulu. Prior to March 20, 2019, the Company&#8217;s ownership share of Hulu results was reported as equity in the loss of investees.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-indent:30px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;"><br></font></div><div><a GUIDANCE</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:32px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">At the beginning of fiscal 2019, the Company adopted new revenue recognition accounting guidance (ASC 606). Results for fiscal 2019 are presented under ASC 606, while prior period amounts continue to be reported in accordance with our historical accounting.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;text-align:left;text-indent:32px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">The current quarter includes a $53 million unfavorable impact on segment operating income from the ASC 606 adoption. The most significant impacts were a $23 million decrease at Parks, Experiences and Products, which reflected the deferral of revenues related to sales of vacation club properties, and a $16 million decrease at Media Networks, which reflected a change in timing of revenue recognition on contracts with minimum guarantees.</font></div><div higher investment impairments. The Company adopted new accounting guidance in fiscal 2019 and now presents the elements of pension and postretirement plan costs, other than service cost, in &#8220;Interest expense, net.&#8221; The comparable benefit of $9 million in the prior-year quarter was reported in &#8220;Costs and expenses.&#8221; The benefit in the current quarter was due to the expected return on pension plan assets exceeding interest expense on plan liabilities and amortization of prior net actuarial losses.</font></div><div><br></div><div><div style="line-height:120%;text-align:center;font-size:10pt;"><font because the measure allows for comparability between periods of the operating performance of the Company&#8217;s business and allows investors to evaluate the impact of these items separately from the The Company uses free cash flow (cash provided by operations less investments in parks, resorts and other property), among other measures, to evaluate the ability of its operations to generate cash that is available for purposes other than capital expenditures. Management believes that information about free cash flow provides investors with an important perspective on the cash available to service debt obligations, make strategic acquisitions and investments and pay dividends or repurchase shares.</font></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;text-decoration:underline;">Aggregate segment operating income</font><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">&#32;&#8211; The Company evaluates the performance of its operating segments based on segment operating income, and management uses aggregate segment operating income as a </font></div><div><br></div><div><div style="line-height:120%;text-align:center;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;">11</font></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div><div style="line-height:120%;font-size:10pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:10pt;"><br></font></div></div><div><br></div><div style="line-height:120%;padding-top:8px;font-size:12pt;"><font style="font-family:inherit;font-size:12pt;">measure of the performance of operating businesses separate from non-operating factors. The Company believes that information about aggregate segment operating income assists investors by allowing them to evaluate changes in the operating results of the Company&#8217;s portfolio of businesses separate from non-operational factors that affect net income, thus providing separate insight into both operations and the other factors that affect reported results. </font></div><div
